## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试，或者帮你查一个网页上的信息。
```

### greet-en

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tu rama local antes de compartirla (ponerla al día con main o limpiar tus commits). Usa merge para integrar una rama que ya es pública o compartida, porque reescribir commits que otros usan causa conflictos en su copia.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 面向连接、保证可靠有序地送达；UDP 不建连接、不保证送达和顺序，但更快、更轻。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own local branch to tidy its commits before anyone else has pulled it. Merge when the branch is shared or already pushed, because rewriting public history breaks anyone who based work on it.
```

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用文件路径就行:

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts
```

Bun 会按路径匹配文件。想只跑某个文件里的一个用例,加 `-t`:

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts -t "用例名字"
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is an append-only file where a database records each change before applying it to the main data. If a crash interrupts a write, the database replays that log on restart to recover committed changes and keep the data consistent.
```

### rich-compare-en

```
--- step 1: user says: Put these side by side for me. Mac mini M4: $599, 16 GB, always plugged in, stays at home. MacBook Air M4: $999, 16 GB, runs on battery, comes with me. Which is the better always-on home for you?
  [partner message] **Mac mini M4.** It costs $400 less, has the same chip and 16 GB, and is built to run 24/7 at home. The Air's main advantage, portability, goes unused if it never leaves the house.

| | Mac mini M4 | MacBook Air M4 |
|---|---|---|
| Price | $599 | $999 |
| Chip and memory | M4, 16 GB | M4, 16 GB |
| Built for always-on | Yes | Works, but it's a laptop |
| Battery | None | Built in. Helps through short outages, but ages faster when kept plugged in 24/7 |
| Wired network | Gigabit Ethernet | No Ethernet, Wi-Fi only |
| Ports | More, including Thunderbolt | Fewer |
| Portability | Stays put | Comes with you |

The one reason to pick the Air is if you want the same machine on the road. For a home base that's always on, the Mini is the better buy.
```

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里提到的锁粒度改细。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户说："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"

要做的：
- 找到 review 里关于锁粒度的意见，把锁的范围缩小到真正需要保护的部分，减少持锁时间和互相阻塞。
- 改动只放在这个 PR 的分支上，提交到该分支，不合并，不推送到主分支，不把 draft 改成 ready。
- 改完跑一遍相关测试，并把结果（通过/失败的测试数）报告出来。
- 如果 review 里的锁粒度意见有多条，逐条说明各自怎么处理了；有哪条没法改，说清楚原因。

范围之外：不要顺手改别的逻辑，不要发评论或回复 review，不要合并或推送。
```

### board-offer-go-zh

```
(earlier) user: 每天早上帮我看一下周五北京到上海的高铁票价，600 以下的告诉我。
(earlier) partner ack: 好，每天早上去看一次。  → task "每天看周五京沪高铁票价" working; its report: 第 3 天：周五 G1 553 元（二等座余 12 张），G3 553 元（余 4 张），G7 626 元；600 以下的有 2 班。
(earlier) partner: 周五 600 以下的还有两班：G1 和 G3，都是 553 元，G3 只剩 4 张。
(earlier) Ducter offered on its own card: keep task "fares"'s rounds on a board?
--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] 做好了，周五京沪高铁的票价都在里面，每天早上更新。
  [an app card under the message: made "周五京沪高铁票价", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1996 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">周五 北京→上海，600 元以下</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="cnt">—</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">每天早上更新</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">今天的班次</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<div class="k-empty" id="empty">还没有数据</div>
<style>
.k-row{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--d-line,#e5e5e5)}
.k-row:last-child{border-bottom:0}
.k-row-main{font-weight:600;color:var(--d-ink,#111)}
.k-row-sub{font-size:13px;color:var(--d-muted,#888)}
.k-row-value{font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;color:var(--d-ink,#111);text-align:right}
.k-row.low .k-row-value{color:var(--d-good,#1a7f37)}
</style>
<script>
function draw(trains){
  var list=document.getElementById('list');
  var empty=document.getElementById('empty');
  list.innerHTML='';
  if(!trains||!trains.length){empty.style.display='block';document.getElementById('cnt').textContent='—';return;}
  empty.style.display='none';
  var low=0;
  trains.forEach(function(t){
    var row=document.createElement('div');
    row.className='k-row'+(t.price<600?' low':'');
    var left=document.createElement('div');
    var main=document.createElement('div');main.className='k-row-main';main.textContent=t.no+'  '+t.from+'→'+t.to+' '+t.time;
    var sub=document.createElement('div');sub.className='k-row-sub';sub.textContent='二等座 余 '+t.seats+' 张';
    left.appendChild(main);left.appendChild(sub);
    var val=document.createElement('div');val.className='k-row-value';val.textContent='¥'+t.price;
    row.appendChild(left);row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
    if(t.price<600)low++;
  });
  document.getElementById('cnt').textContent=low+' 班';
  var checked=ducter.get('checked');
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent=checked?('更新于 '+checked):'每天早上更新';
}
draw(ducter.get('trains'));
ducter.onChange(function(){draw(ducter.get('trains'));});
</script>

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="w"><span class="k-none">Not yet</span></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="s">The first report lands here.</div>
</div>

<script>
function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c];});}
function render(){
  var nights = ducter.get('nights') || [];
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
  if (!nights.length) { list.innerHTML = ''; empty.hidden = false; return; }
  empty.hidden = true;
  var last = nights[nights.length - 1];
  document.getElementById('w').textContent = last.writes + ' writes/s';
  document.getElementById('s').textContent = last.night + ' · p99 ' + last.p99;
  list.innerHTML = nights.slice().reverse().map(function(n){
    return '<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">' + esc(n.night) +
      '<div class="k-row-sub">Compaction stalls: ' + esc(n.stalls) + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-value">' + esc(n.writes) + '/s<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + esc(n.p99) + '</div></div></div>';
  }).join('');
  ducter.summary(last.night + ': ' + last.writes + ' writes/s, p99 ' + last.p99);
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();

  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</div>

<style>
.k-sum-value{font-size:44px;font-weight:700;line-height:1.1;color:var(--d-ink)}
.k-sum-label{font-size:15px;color:var(--d-muted)}

.k-sum{background:var(--d-card);border-radius:16px;padding:20px 18px;margin-bottom:16px;text-align:left}
</style>
<script>
function draw(){
  const xs = ducter.get("expenses") || [];
  let total = 0;
  const list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  for (const x of xs) {
    total += Number(x.yuan) || 0;
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "k-row";
    const main = document.createElement("span");
    main.className = "k-row-main";
    main.textContent = x.what;
    const sub = document.createElement("span");
    sub.className = "k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent = x.day;
    const val = document.createElement("span");
    val.className = "k-row-value";
    val.textContent = "¥" + x.yuan;
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(sub);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  document.getElementById("total").textContent = "¥" + total;
  document.getElementById("count").textContent = xs.length ? xs.length + " 笔" : "还没记";
  ducter.summary(xs.length ? xs.length + " 笔,合计 ¥" + total : "还没记");
}
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

    if (v === null) return;

    document.querySelector('.k-add').open = true;

    return null;
  }
  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();

<style>
  .k-app { color: var(--d-ink); background: var(--d-bg); }
  .pr-row { display: flex; align-items: flex-start; gap: 12px; cursor: pointer; }
  .pr-box { flex: 0 0 22px; height: 22px; margin-top: 2px; border-radius: 50%; border: 1.5px solid var(--d-line); display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-size: 13px; color: var(--d-bg); background: transparent; }
  .pr-row[aria-pressed="true"] .pr-box { background: var(--d-good); border-color: var(--d-good); }
  .pr-row[aria-pressed="true"] .k-row-main { text-decoration: line-through; color: var(--d-muted); }
  .pr-row[aria-pressed="true"] .k-row-sub { color: var(--d-muted); }
  .pr-ref { font-family: ui-monospace, Menlo, monospace; font-size: 12px; color: var(--d-muted); }
</style>

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">of 4 PRs · tap one when it lands</div>
</div>

<h2 class="k-h">Open PRs</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</div>
</main>

<script>
  var PRS = ducter.get("prs") || [];
  var landed = {};

  function render() {
    var list = document.getElementById("list");
    list.innerHTML = "";
    PRS.forEach(function (p) {
      var done = !!landed[p.id];
      var row = document.createElement("div");
      row.className = "k-row pr-row";
      row.setAttribute("aria-pressed", done ? "true" : "false");
      row.innerHTML =
        '<span class="pr-box">' + (done ? "✓" : "") + '</span>' +
        '<div style="flex:1">' +
          '<div class="k-row-main">' + p.title + '</div>' +
          '<div class="k-row-sub">' + p.sub + '</div>' +
          '<div class="pr-ref">' + p.ref + '</div>' +
        '</div>';
      row.addEventListener("click", function () {
        landed[p.id] = !landed[p.id];
        ducter.set("landed", landed);
        render();
      });
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    var waiting = PRS.filter(function (p) { return !landed[p.id]; }).length;
    document.getElementById("count").textContent = String(waiting);
    document.getElementById("sub").textContent = "of " + PRS.length + " PRs · tap one when it lands";
    ducter.summary(waiting + " waiting");
  }

  function load() {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get("landed")).then(function (v) {
      landed = v || {};
      render();
    });
  }

  render();
  load();
  ducter.onChange(load);
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter-412","ref":"wey-gu/ducter#412","title":"Compaction cut","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","ref":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","title":"Retry on 502","sub":"On CI, currently failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","ref":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","ref":"wey-gu/ducter#418","title":"Apps bar","sub":"Draft"}]}
